# Backend Business Analysis
- Files scanned: **325**
- Controllers: **44**
- Services: **54**
- DTOs: **91**

## Routes

- **GET** `/` → `getHello`
- **GET** `/health` → `getHealth`
- **GET** `/admin/dashboard/metrics` → `getMetrics`
- **GET** `/admin/dashboard/revenue-trend` → `getRevenueTrend`
- **GET** `/admin/queues/status` → `getQueuesStatus`
- **GET** `/admin/queues/:queueName/jobs` → `getJobs`

        "@HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)",
        "@Roles(UserRole.PROVIDER)",
        "@ApiBearerAuth()",
        "@ApiOperation({",
        "@ApiResponse({",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Não autorizado.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({",
        "@Roles(UserRole.ADMIN)",
        "@ApiOperation({",
        "@ApiResponse({",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Dados inválidos.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Não autorizado.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Provedor não encontrado.' })",
        "@Roles(UserRole.ADMIN)",
        "@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Rejeitar um provedor e fornecer um motivo' })",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Provedor rejeitado com sucesso.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Não autorizado.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Acesso proibido.' })",
        "@ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Provedor não encontrado.' })"
      ]
    }
  ]
}



// Este arquivo é o ponto de entrada para o seu banco de dados.
// Ele define os modelos de dados e como eles se relacionam.

generator client {
  provider        = "prisma-client-js"
  previewFeatures = ["postgresqlExtensions"]
  // ADICIONADO: Configuração para binaryTargets para resolver o problema de libssl no Docker
  binaryTargets   = ["native", "debian-openssl-1.1.x", "debian-openssl-3.0.x"]
}

datasource db {
  provider   = "postgresql" // Ou "mysql", "sqlite", etc., dependendo do seu DB
  url        = env("DATABASE_URL")
  // ADICIONADO: Habilitar extensão PostGIS para funcionalidades geoespaciais
  extensions = [postgis]
}

// ---------------------------------
// Enums
// ---------------------------------
enum UserRole {
  CLIENT
  PROVIDER
  ADMIN
  SYSTEM        // Papel para operações internas do sistema (ex: webhooks)
  SUPPORT_AGENT // Papel para agentes de suporte
}

enum VerificationStatus {
  PENDING_INITIAL_REVIEW
  PENDING_DOCUMENTS_UPLOAD
  PENDING_BACKGROUND_CHECK
  PENDING_MANUAL_REVIEW
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
  BLOCKED
}

enum PricingType {
  FIXED_PRICE
  HOURLY
  BY_SIZE
  CUSTOM_QUOTE
}

enum TransactionStatus {
  PENDING
  PAID
  FAILED
  CANCELED
  REFUNDED
  PROCESSED 
  COMPLETED
}

enum BookingStatus {
  PENDING
  PENDING_PROVIDER_CONFIRMATION
  CONFIRMED
  ON_THE_WAY    // Prestador a caminho
  ARRIVED       // Prestador chegou
  STARTED       // Serviço iniciado
  FINISHED      // Serviço finalizado
  CANCELED
  PENDING_DISPUTE
  RESCHEDULED
  REJECTED
  NO_SHOW
}

enum TransactionType {
  PAYMENT
  WITHDRAWAL
  COMMISSION
  REFUND
}

enum PaymentIntentStatus {
  PENDING
  PAID
  EXPIRED
  REFUNDED
  CHARGEBACK
}

enum PixKeyType {
  CPF
  CNPJ
  EMAIL
  PHONE
  RANDOM
}

enum DisputeReason {
  SERVICE_NOT_PERFORMED
  SERVICE_INCOMPLETE
  QUALITY_ISSUES
  PROVIDER_DID_NOT_SHOW
  CLIENT_DID_NOT_SHOW
  OTHER
}

enum DisputeStatus {
  PENDING
  IN_REVIEW
  RESOLVED
  REJECTED
}

enum SupportTicketStatus {
  OPEN
  IN_PROGRESS
  WAITING_USER
  RESOLVED
  CLOSED
  ESCALATED
}

enum SupportTicketCategory {
  PAYMENT
  QUALITY
  APP
  OTHER
}

enum SubscriptionStatus {
  ACTIVE
  PAUSED
  CANCELED
  COMPLETED
}

enum SubscriptionFrequency {
  WEEKLY
  BI_WEEKLY
  MONTHLY
}

enum IncidentType {
  DAMAGE
  MISCONDUCT
  THEFT
  NO_SHOW
  OTHER
}

enum IncidentStatus {
  PENDING_REVIEW
  INVESTIGATING
  RESOLVED
  REJECTED
}

enum CouponType {
  PERCENT
  FIXED
}

enum CouponTarget {
  GENERAL
  NEW_CLIENTS
  SPECIFIC_SERVICE
  SPECIFIC_PROVIDER
  NEW_CUSTOMER
  REFERRAL_REFERRED
  REFERRAL_REFERRER
  MISSION_REWARD
  REPEAT_CUSTOMER
}

enum CouponStatus {
  ACTIVE
  INACTIVE
  EXPIRED
  USED_UP
}

enum ClaimStatus {
  PENDING
  UNDER_REVIEW
  APPROVED
  REJECTED
  SETTLED
}

enum LoyaltyTransactionType {
  SERVICE_COMPLETED
  REVIEW_SUBMITTED
  FIRST_REVIEW
  REFERRAL
  REDEEM
  MISSION_COMPLETED
  ADMIN_ADJUSTMENT
  REFERRAL_CONVERSION
  PROFILE_COMPLETION
}

// ====== MISSIONS ======
enum MissionAudience {
  CLIENT
  PROVIDER
  GENERAL
}

enum MissionKind {
  COUNT_EVENT
  STREAK_DAYS
  WITHIN_WINDOW
}

enum RewardType {
  COUPON
  POINTS
}

enum MissionStatus {
  ACTIVE
  COMPLETED
  CLAIMED
}
// ====== FIM MISSIONS ======

enum OfferTarget {
  GENERAL
  SPECIFIC_SERVICE
  SPECIFIC_PROVIDER
  NEW_CLIENTS
}

enum OfferStatus {
  ACTIVE
  INACTIVE
  EXPIRED
}

// ADICIONADOS (sem quebrar o atual)
enum LedgerEntryType {
  EARNING
  ADJUSTMENT
  WITHDRAWAL
  FEE
  REFUND
  HOLD
  RELEASE
}

enum PayoutStatus {
  PENDING
  PROCESSING
  PAID
  FAILED
  CANCELED
}

// (Opcional) Escopo de pricing – adicional ao modelo atual
enum PricingScope {
  GLOBAL
  CITY
  CATEGORY
  PROVIDER
}

// ---------------------------------
// Models
// ---------------------------------
model User {
  id                    String             @id @default(uuid())
  email                 String             @unique
  phone                 String?            @unique
  passwordHash          String?
  role                  UserRole           @default(CLIENT)
  avatarUrl             String?
  firebaseUid           String?            @unique
  fullName              String             @default("Nome Padrão")
  fcmToken              String?            @unique
  isPhoneVerified       Boolean            @default(false)
  isVerified            Boolean            @default(false)
  deletionScheduledAt   DateTime?
  createdAt             DateTime           @default(now())
  updatedAt             DateTime           @updatedAt
  preferredLanguage     String?
  myReferralCode        String?            @unique

  client                Client?
  provider              Provider?
  messagesSent          Message[]          @relation("SentMessages")
  messagesReceived      Message[]          @relation("ReceivedMessages")
  notifications         Notification[]
  chatsAsParticipant1   Chat[]             @relation("ChatParticipant1")
  chatsAsParticipant2   Chat[]             @relation("ChatParticipant2")
  referredBy            Referral[]         @relation("ReferredByUser")
  referralsMade         Referral[]         @relation("ReferrerOfUser")
  disputesReported      Dispute[]          @relation("DisputeReporter")
  reportedIncidents     Incident[]         @relation("ReportedIncidents")
  panicAlerts           PanicAlert[]       @relation("PanicAlerts")
  userConsents          UserConsent[]
  disputeMessagesSent   DisputeMessage[]   @relation("DisputeSender")
  loyalty               Loyalty?
  loyaltyTransactions   LoyaltyTransaction[]
  couponUsages          CouponUsage[]
  issuedCoupons         Coupon[]           @relation("IssuedCoupons")

  // MISSIONS
  missionProgress       MissionProgress[]
  missionEvents         MissionEvent[]

  // SUPORTE
  supportTickets        SupportTicket[]
  supportMessages       SupportMessage[]
  assignedTickets       SupportTicket[]    @relation("AssignedTickets")

  // ADICIONADOS (ledger/payout)
  ledgerEntries         LedgerEntry[]
  payouts               Payout[]

  // CORREÇÃO: Adicionado para relação bilateral com CouponReservation
  couponReservations    CouponReservation[]

  // ADICIONADO: Relações para auditoria de Booking (quem iniciou/finalizou)
  startedBookings       Booking[]          @relation("BookingStartedBy")
  completedBookings     Booking[]          @relation("BookingCompletedBy")
}

model Client {
  id                      String           @id @default(uuid())
  userId                  String           @unique
  user                    User             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  fullName                String
  completedBookingsCount  Int              @default(0)
  phone                   String?
  cpf                     String?          @unique
  dateOfBirth             DateTime?
  address                 Address?         @relation("ClientAddress")
  bookings                Booking[]
  reviewsMade             Review[]         @relation("ClientReviews")
  noShowCount             Int              @default(0)
  cancellationCount       Int              @default(0)
  subscriptions           Subscription[]
  guaranteeClaims         GuaranteeClaim[]
  createdAt               DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt               DateTime         @updatedAt
}
  

model Address {
  id           String    @id @default(uuid())
  cep          String
  street       String
  number       String
  complement   String?
  neighborhood String
  city         String
  state        String
  clientId     String?   @unique  // Mantido @unique para 1 endereço por client (intencional); troque para @@index se múltiplos endereços planejados
  providerId   String?   @unique  // Mantido @unique para 1 endereço por provider (intencional); troque para @@index se múltiplos endereços planejados
  client       Client?   @relation("ClientAddress", fields: [clientId], references: [id])
  provider     Provider? @relation("ProviderAddress", fields: [providerId], references: [id])
  booking      Booking?  @relation("BookingAddress")
  // Geo: Mantidos ambos para compatibilidade; normalizar na aplicação para evitar divergência (ex: derivar lat/lon de location ou vice-versa)
  location     Unsupported("geometry(Point, 4326)")?
  latitude     Float?
  longitude    Float?
}

model Service {
  id                  String            @id @default(uuid())
  name                String            @unique
  description         String?
  // DEPRECATED: preço no nível do serviço global
  // Tornado opcional para tratar Serviço como categoria/tipo
  price               Decimal?          @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  defaultPricingType  PricingType?
  icon                String?
  providerServices    ProviderService[]
  createdAt           DateTime          @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime          @updatedAt
}

// MODELO AUSENTE: Provider
// Adicionado para resolver erros de validação (P1012) nas relações que referenciam Provider
model Provider {
  id                      String              @id @default(uuid())
  userId                  String              @unique
  user                    User                @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  // Dados públicos do provedor
  fullName                String
  phone                   String?
  bio                     String?
  yearsOfExperience       Int?
  avatarUrl               String?
  verificationStatus      VerificationStatus  @default(PENDING_INITIAL_REVIEW)

  // Documentos e verificação
  cpf                     String?             @unique
  dateOfBirth             DateTime?
  documentPhotoFrontUrl   String?
  documentPhotoBackUrl    String?
  selfieWithDocumentUrl   String?
  backgroundCheckResult   Json?
  rejectionReason         String?
  ocrResult               Json?
  livenessResult          Json?

  // Métricas/opcionais usados nos cards
  acceptanceRate          Int?
  averageResponseTime     Int?
  fiveStarReviewCount     Int                @default(0)
  monthlyBookingsCount    Int                @default(0)
  badges                  String[]

  // Pagamentos (PIX)
  pixKey                  String?
  pixKeyMasked            String?

  // Relacionamentos
  address                 Address?           @relation("ProviderAddress")
  providerServices        ProviderService[]
  bookings                Booking[]
  reviewsReceived         Review[]           @relation("ProviderReviews")
  availability            Availability[]
  transactions            Transaction[]
  // Back-relations requeridos
  subscriptions           Subscription[]
  guaranteeClaims         GuaranteeClaim[]

  createdAt               DateTime           @default(now())
  updatedAt               DateTime           @updatedAt

  @@index([verificationStatus, yearsOfExperience])
}

model ProviderService {
  id                    String         @id @default(uuid())
  providerId            String
  serviceId             String
  price                 Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  pricePerHour          Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  pricePerSquareMeter   Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  pricePerRoom          Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  durationMinutes       Int?
  description           String?
  provider              Provider       @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  pricingType           PricingType    @default(FIXED_PRICE)
  service               Service        @relation(fields: [serviceId], references: [id])
  bookings              Booking[]
  createdAt             DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt             DateTime       @updatedAt
  subscriptions         Subscription[] @relation("ProviderServiceSubscriptions")
  versions              ProviderServiceVersion[] // Histórico de preço/tipo; inserir versão em toda alteração na aplicação

  @@unique([providerId, serviceId, pricingType])
}

model ProviderServiceVersion {
  id                 String          @id @default(uuid())
  providerServiceId  String
  providerService    ProviderService @relation(fields: [providerServiceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  price              Decimal         @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  pricingType        PricingType
  note               String?
  changedAt          DateTime        @default(now())

  @@index([providerServiceId, changedAt])
}

model Booking {
  id               String          @id @default(uuid())
  clientId         String
  providerId       String
  providerServiceId String

  client           Client          @relation(fields: [clientId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  provider         Provider        @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  providerService  ProviderService @relation(fields: [providerServiceId], references: [id])

  // Agendamento
  scheduledDate    DateTime
  scheduledTime    String
  scheduledStart   DateTime?
  durationMinutes  Int?
  scheduledEnd     DateTime?        // ✅ NOVO: derivado (scheduledStart + durationMinutes)
  status           BookingStatus    @default(PENDING)

  // Preço e descontos
  totalPrice       Decimal          @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  discountAmount   Decimal?         @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  notes            String?

  // Timestamps de progresso (FLUXO COMPLETO)
  onTheWayAt       DateTime?        // Quando prestador ativou "a caminho"
  arrivedAt        DateTime?        // Quando prestador ativou "cheguei"
  startedAt        DateTime?        // Quando serviço foi iniciado
  completedAt      DateTime?        // Quando serviço foi finalizado

  // Auditoria (quem fez a ação)
  startedByUserId    String?
  completedByUserId  String?
  startedByUser      User?          @relation("BookingStartedBy", fields: [startedByUserId], references: [id])
  completedByUser    User?          @relation("BookingCompletedBy", fields: [completedByUserId], references: [id])

  // Relacionamentos
  review           Review?
  isReviewed       Boolean          @default(false)
  transactions     Transaction[]
  addressId        String?          @unique
  address          Address?         @relation("BookingAddress", fields: [addressId], references: [id])
  dispute          Dispute?
  subscriptionId   String?
  subscription     Subscription?    @relation("SubscriptionBookings", fields: [subscriptionId], references: [id])
  incidents        Incident[]
  guaranteeClaims  GuaranteeClaim[]
  couponId         String?
  coupon           Coupon?          @relation(fields: [couponId], references: [id])
  couponUsage      CouponUsage?     @relation("BookingCouponUsage")
  supportTickets   SupportTicket[]
  paymentIntent    PaymentIntent?
  ledgerEntries    LedgerEntry[]

  // Metadata
  createdAt        DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime         @updatedAt

  // Índices para performance
  @@index([providerId, status, scheduledDate])
  @@index([clientId, status, scheduledDate])
  @@index([status, scheduledDate])
  @@index([providerId, scheduledStart, scheduledEnd]) // ✅ NOVO: overlap fast
}


model Chat {
  id                String    @id @default(uuid())
  participant1Id    String
  participant1      User      @relation("ChatParticipant1", fields: [participant1Id], references: [id])
  participant2Id    String
  participant2      User      @relation("ChatParticipant2", fields: [participant2Id], references: [id])
  messages          Message[]
  createdAt         DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@unique([participant1Id, participant2Id])
}

model Message {
  id         String   @id @default(uuid())
  chatId     String
  chat       Chat     @relation(fields: [chatId], references: [id])
  senderId   String
  sender     User     @relation("SentMessages", fields: [senderId], references: [id])
  receiverId String
  receiver   User     @relation("ReceivedMessages", fields: [receiverId], references: [id])
  content    String
  timestamp  DateTime @default(now())
  isRead     Boolean  @default(false)
  targetUrl  String?
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
}

model Notification {
  id               String   @id @default(uuid())
  userId           String
  user             User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  type             String
  message          String
  isRead           Boolean  @default(false)
  targetUrl        String?
  createdAt        DateTime @default(now())
  imageUrl         String?
  actionButtons    Json?
  title            String?
  category         String?

  // ADICIONADO: Ajustes recomendados para notificações (mínimo viável: idempotência, agendamento, prioridade e readAt)
  idempotencyKey   String?  @unique  // Opcional; popular sempre (especialmente de webhooks/queues) para prevenir duplicados (Postgres permite múltiplos NULL)
  scheduledAt      DateTime?
  priority         Int?     @default(2)
  readAt           DateTime?

  // ADICIONADO: Índice para performance (listagem ordenada por user/createdAt)
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  // ADICIONADO: Índice para polling por priority (recomendado para envelopes em prioridade)
  @@index([userId, priority, createdAt])
}

model Review {
  id         String   @id @default(uuid())
  booking    Booking  @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  bookingId  String   @unique
  clientId   String
  providerId String
  rating     Int
  comment    String?
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt  DateTime @updatedAt
  client     Client   @relation("ClientReviews", fields: [clientId], references: [id])
  provider   Provider @relation("ProviderReviews", fields: [providerId], references: [id])

  @@unique([bookingId, clientId, providerId])
}

model Offer {
  id                  String       @id @default(uuid())
  title               String
  description         String?
  discountPercentage  Float?
  fixedDiscountAmount Decimal?
  validFrom           DateTime?
  validUntil          DateTime
  imageUrl            String?
  target              OfferTarget
  targetId            String?
  status              OfferStatus
  createdAt           DateTime     @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime     @updatedAt
}

model Transaction {
  id                   String            @id @default(uuid())
  providerId           String
  provider             Provider          @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  amount               Decimal           @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  type                 TransactionType
  status               TransactionStatus @default(PENDING)
  description          String?
  createdAt            DateTime          @default(now())

  bookingId            String?
  booking              Booking?          @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])

  gatewayTransactionId String?           @unique
  qrCodeUrl            String?
  transactionRef       String?

  couponId             String?
  coupon               Coupon?           @relation(fields: [couponId], references: [id])

  pixKeyType           PixKeyType?
  pixKey               String?           // valor sensível
  pixKeyMasked         String?           // versão mascarada para exibição segura

  @@index([providerId, status, createdAt])
}


model PaymentIntent {
  id                 String              @id @default(uuid())
  bookingId          String              @unique
  booking            Booking             @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  amountCents        Int
  status             PaymentIntentStatus @default(PENDING)
  gateway            String              @default("PAGSEGURO_ORDER_PIX")
  externalRef        String?
  externalOrderId    String?
  externalChargeId   String?
  externalQrCodeId   String?
  referenceId        String?
  idempotencyKey     String?             @unique  // Garantir uso em API/seed para idempotência
  createdAt          DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt          DateTime            @updatedAt
  expiresAt          DateTime?
  qrCodeUrl          String?
  qrCodeText         String?
  paymentEvents      PaymentEvent[]

  // ADICIONADO: Índice para job de limpeza de intents vencidos (por status/updatedAt)
  @@index([status, updatedAt])
}

model PaymentEvent {
  id              String        @id @default(uuid())
  paymentIntentId String
  paymentIntent   PaymentIntent @relation(fields: [paymentIntentId], references: [id])
  type            String
  payload         Json?
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
}

model Availability {
  id          String   @id @default(uuid())
  providerId  String
  provider    Provider @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  dayOfWeek   Int
  startTime   String
  endTime     String
  isAvailable Boolean  @default(true)

  @@index([providerId, dayOfWeek])
}

model FAQItem {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  question  String   @unique
  answer    String
  category  String?
  order     Int      @default(0)
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Referral {
  id             String   @id @default(uuid())
  referredUserId String   @unique
  referredUser   User     @relation("ReferredByUser", fields: [referredUserId], references: [id])
  referrerUserId String
  referrerUser   User     @relation("ReferrerOfUser", fields: [referrerUserId], references: [id])
  referralCode   String?
  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([referredUserId, referrerUserId])
}

model Dispute {
  id                   String         @id @default(uuid())
  bookingId            String         @unique
  booking              Booking        @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  reporterUserId       String
  reporterUser         User           @relation("DisputeReporter", fields: [reporterUserId], references: [id])
  reason               DisputeReason
  description          String
  refundAmountProposed Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  attachments          String[]
  status               DisputeStatus  @default(PENDING)
  resolutionNotes      String?
  resolvedByUserId     String?
  resolvedAt           DateTime?
  createdAt            DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt            DateTime       @updatedAt
  messages             DisputeMessage[] @relation("DisputeMessages")

  // CORREÇÃO: Adicionado para relação bilateral com LedgerEntry
  ledgerEntries        LedgerEntry[]
}

model DisputeMessage {
  id           String        @id @default(uuid())
  ticketId     String
  ticket       SupportTicket @relation("DisputeMessageTicket", fields: [ticketId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  senderUserId String
  sender       User          @relation("DisputeSender", fields: [senderUserId], references: [id])
  content      String
  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  disputeId    String
  dispute      Dispute       @relation("DisputeMessages", fields: [disputeId], references: [id])
}

model UserConsent {
  userId       String
  user         User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  documentType String
  version      String
  consentedAt  DateTime @default(now())

  @@id([userId, documentType])
}

model Subscription {
  id                   String               @id @default(uuid())
  clientId             String
  client               Client               @relation(fields: [clientId], references: [id])
  providerId           String
  provider             Provider             @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id])
  providerServiceId    String
  providerService      ProviderService      @relation("ProviderServiceSubscriptions", fields: [providerServiceId], references: [id])
  frequency            SubscriptionFrequency
  startDate            DateTime
  endDate              DateTime?
  status               SubscriptionStatus   @default(ACTIVE)
  totalPrice           Decimal              @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  nextGenerationDate   DateTime
  generatedBookings    Booking[]            @relation("SubscriptionBookings")
  createdAt            DateTime             @default(now())
  updatedAt            DateTime             @updatedAt
}

model Incident {
  id          String         @id @default(uuid())
  reporterId  String
  reporter    User           @relation("ReportedIncidents", fields: [reporterId], references: [id])
  bookingId   String?
  booking     Booking?       @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  type        IncidentType
  description String
  attachments String[]
  status      IncidentStatus @default(PENDING_REVIEW)
  resolution  String?
  resolvedBy  String?
  resolvedAt  DateTime?
  createdAt   DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime       @updatedAt
}

model PanicAlert {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  userId    String
  user      User     @relation("PanicAlerts", fields: [userId], references: [id])
  latitude  Decimal  @db.Decimal(10, 8)
  longitude Decimal  @db.Decimal(11, 8)
  message   String?
  status    String   @default("ACTIVE")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
}

// CUPONS
model Coupon {
  id               String         @id @default(uuid())
  code             String         @unique
  description      String?
  value            Decimal        @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  valueType        CouponType
  target           CouponTarget
  targetId         String?
  maxUses          Int?
  usesCount        Int            @default(0)
  validFrom        DateTime
  validUntil       DateTime
  status           CouponStatus   @default(ACTIVE)
  createdAt        DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime       @updatedAt
  usages           CouponUsage[]
  bookings         Booking[]
  transactions     Transaction[]
  maxDiscount      Decimal?       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  firstBookingOnly Boolean        @default(false)
  issuedToUserId   String?
  issuedToUser     User?          @relation("IssuedCoupons", fields: [issuedToUserId], references: [id])
  issuedBy         String?
  reservations     CouponReservation[]  // ADICIONADO
}

model CouponUsage {
  id           String   @id @default(uuid())
  couponId     String
  coupon       Coupon   @relation(fields: [couponId], references: [id])
  userId       String
  user         User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  bookingId    String   @unique
  booking      Booking  @relation("BookingCouponUsage", fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  appliedValue Decimal  @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
}

model CouponReservation {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  couponId   String
  coupon     Coupon   @relation(fields: [couponId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  userId     String
  user       User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  bookingId  String?
  expiresAt  DateTime
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([couponId, userId, bookingId])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model GuaranteeClaim {
  id             String      @id @default(uuid())
  bookingId      String
  booking        Booking     @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  clientId       String
  client         Client      @relation(fields: [clientId], references: [id])
  providerId     String
  provider       Provider    @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id])
  description    String
  attachments    String[]
  estimatedValue Decimal?    @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  resolvedValue  Decimal?    @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  status         ClaimStatus @default(PENDING)
  resolutionNotes String?
  resolvedAt     DateTime?
  createdAt      DateTime    @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime    @updatedAt
}

// PRICING (mantém campos atuais; acrescenta escopo opcional)
model PricingRule {
  id              String    @id @default(uuid())
  zoneId          String?
  dayOfWeek       Int?
  startTime       String?
  endTime         String?
  activeFrom      DateTime?
  activeTo        DateTime?
  demandThreshold Int?
  surgeFactor     Decimal   @db.Decimal(3, 2)
  isActive        Boolean   @default(true)
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt

  // Novos campos opcionais (não quebram o existente)
  scope           PricingScope?
  cityCode        String?
  categoryId      String?
  providerId      String?
  maxMultiplier   Decimal?  @db.Decimal(3, 2)

  @@index([scope, isActive, cityCode, categoryId, providerId])
}

model Loyalty {
  id            String   @id @default(uuid())
  userId        String   @unique
  currentPoints Int      @default(0)
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt     DateTime @updatedAt
  user          User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
}

model LoyaltyTransaction {
  id          String               @id @default(uuid())
  userId      String
  user        User                 @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  points      Int
  type        LoyaltyTransactionType
  referenceId String?
  createdAt   DateTime             @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, type, referenceId])
}

model Reward {
  id          String   @id @default(uuid())
  name        String   @unique
  description String?
  costPoints  Int
  value       Decimal  @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  type        String   // COUPON, CASHBACK...
  couponCode  String?
  isActive    Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime @updatedAt
}

// ====== MISSIONS ======
model Mission {
  id               String         @id @default(uuid())
  code             String         @unique
  title            String
  description      String
  audience         MissionAudience @default(CLIENT)
  kind             MissionKind
  eventName        String
  targetValue      Int
  timeWindowDays   Int?
  rewardType       RewardType
  rewardValue      Int
  couponTemplateId String?
  isActive         Boolean        @default(true)
  createdAt        DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime       @updatedAt

  progress         MissionProgress[]
}

model MissionProgress {
  id           String        @id @default(uuid())
  userId       String
  missionId    String
  currentValue Int           @default(0)
  status       MissionStatus @default(ACTIVE)
  lastEventAt  DateTime?
  completedAt  DateTime?
  claimedAt    DateTime?

  mission      Mission       @relation(fields: [missionId], references: [id])
  user         User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])

  @@unique([userId, missionId])
}

model MissionEvent {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  userId    String
  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  name      String
  meta      Json?
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
}
// ====== FIM MISSIONS ======

model SupportTicket {
  id             String              @id @default(uuid())
  userId         String
  user           User                @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  role           UserRole
  subject        String
  category       SupportTicketCategory
  description    String
  bookingId      String?
  booking        Booking?            @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  status         SupportTicketStatus @default(OPEN)
  assignedToId   String?
  assignedTo     User?               @relation("AssignedTickets", fields: [assignedToId], references: [id])
  createdAt      DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime            @updatedAt
  closedAt       DateTime?

  messages       SupportMessage[]
  slaLogs        SupportSlaLog[]
  disputeMessages DisputeMessage[]   @relation("DisputeMessageTicket")
}

model SupportMessage {
  id          String        @id @default(uuid())
  ticketId    String
  ticket      SupportTicket @relation(fields: [ticketId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  userId      String
  user        User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id])
  role        UserRole
  body        String
  attachments String[]
  createdAt   DateTime      @default(now())
}

model SupportSlaLog {
  id         String              @id @default(uuid())
  ticketId   String
  ticket     SupportTicket       @relation(fields: [ticketId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  fromStatus SupportTicketStatus
  toStatus   SupportTicketStatus
  createdAt  DateTime            @default(now())
}

// ====== ADIÇÕES PARA PAYOUTS/LEDGER ======
model LedgerEntry {
  id        String          @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String
  user      User            @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  bookingId String?
  booking   Booking?        @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id])
  disputeId String?
  dispute   Dispute?        @relation(fields: [disputeId], references: [id])
  amount    Decimal         @db.Decimal(14, 2) // +credit / -debit
  type      LedgerEntryType
  note      String?
  createdAt DateTime        @default(now())

  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  // ADICIONADO: Índice para consultas frequentes por bookingId (conciliação mais eficiente)
  @@index([bookingId])
}

model Payout {
  id             String       @id @default(cuid())
  userId         String
  user           User         @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  amount         Decimal      @db.Decimal(14, 2)
  status         PayoutStatus @default(PENDING)
  gatewayTxnId   String?      @unique
  idempotencyKey String       @unique  // Garantir uso em API/seed para idempotência
  requestedAt    DateTime     @default(now())
  processedAt    DateTime?
}

model WebhookReplay {
  id      String   @id @default(cuid())
  source  String
  eventId String
  seenAt  DateTime @default(now())
}
